data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# Retrieve BIOS information\nGet-CimInstance -ClassName Win32_BIOS\n# Retrieve information about locally connected physical disk devices\nGet-CimInstance -ClassName Win32_DiskDrive\n# Retrieve information about install physical memory (RAM)\nGet-CimInstance -ClassName Win32_PhysicalMemory\n# Retrieve information about installed network adapters (physical + virtual)\nGet-CimInstance -ClassName Win32_NetworkAdapter\n# Retrieve information about installed graphics / video card (GPU)\nGet-CimInstance -ClassName Win32_VideoController\n\n# List all the classNames\nGet-CimClass | Select-Object -ExpandProperty CimClassName\n# Explore the various WMI classes available in the root\\cimv2 namespace\nGet-CimClass -Namespace root\\cimv2\n# Explore the child WMI namespaces underneath the root\\cimv2 namespace\nGet-CimInstance -Namespace root -ClassName __NAMESPACE\n\n\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# Retrieve local user account information\nGet-LocalUser\n\n# Create a new local user account\nNew-LocalUser -Name NewUser -Password (ConvertTo-SecureString \u0026quot;Password123\u0026quot; -AsPlainText -Force)\n\n# Remove a local user account\nRemove-LocalUser -Name UserToRemove\n\n# Retrieve local group data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"function Get-EvenNumbers {\n    [CmdletBinding()]\n    param (\n        [Parameter(ValueFromPipeline = $true)]\n        [int] $Number\n    )\n    begin {\u0026lt;#command#\u0026gt;}\n    process {\n        if ($Number % 2 -eq 0) {\n            Write-Output $Number\n        }\n    }\n    end {\u0026lt;#command#\u0026gt;}\n}\n1..10 | data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# PowerShell looks module in the path\n$env:PSModulePath\n\n# lists all modules installed on system\nGet-Module -ListAvailable\n# modules imported into current session\nGet-Module\n\nImport-Module \u0026lt;moduleName\u0026gt;\nRemove-Module \u0026lt;moduleName\u0026gt;\n\nFind-Module -Tag cloud\nFind-Module -Name ps*\n\n# Create an in-memory PowerShell module\nNew-Module -Name trevor -ScriptBlock {\n  function Add($a,$b) { $a + $b } }\n\n\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e PowerShell","date: 2020-11-25 18:28:43","background: bg-[#397fe4]","tags:","  - script","  - windows","categories:","  - Programming","  - Operating System","intro: This is a quick reference cheat sheet to getting started with PowerShell scripting.","plugins:","  - copyCode","---","","## Basic Commands","","### Helper Commands","","**_Powershell follows Verb-Noun format for their commands._**","","Some common Verbs:","","| Verb    | Description                                       |","| ------- | ------------------------------------------------- |","| Get     | Used to retrieve information.                     |","| Set     | Used to configure or change settings.             |","| New     | Used to create new instances of objects.          |","| Remove  | Used to delete or remove items.                   |","| Invoke  | Used to execute a specific action or operation.   |","| Start   | Used to initiate a process or operation.          |","| Stop    | Used to halt or terminate a process or operation. |","| Enable  | Used to activate or enable a feature.             |","| Disable | Used to deactivate or disable a feature.          |","| Test    | Used to perform tests or checks.                  |","| Update  | Used to update or refresh data or configurations. |","","Lists available modules","","```powershell","Get-Module --ListAvailable","```","","Lists available cmdlets and functions.","","```powershell","Get-Command -Module ActiveDirectory","```","","Retrieves help","","```powershell","Get-Help \u003ccmd\u003e","Get-Help \u003ccmd\u003e -Examples","Get-Help -Name Get-Process -Parameter Id","```","","Lists aliases and their corresponding cmdlet names.","","```powershell","Get-Alias | Select-Object Name, Definition","```","","**Get-Member:** Displays the properties and methods of objects.","","```powershell","Get-Process | Get-Member","```","","### Object Manipulation {.col-span-2}","","**Select-Object:** Selects specific properties from objects or customizes their display.","","```powershell","Get-Process | Select-Object Name, CPU","```","","**Where-Object:** Filters objects based on specified conditions.","","```powershell","Get-Service | Where-Object { $PSItem.Status -eq 'Running' }","#OR","Get-Service | ? { $_.Status -eq 'Running' }","```","","**Measure-Object:** Calculates statistics, like sum, average, and count, for object properties.","","```powershell","Get-Process | Measure-Object -Property WorkingSet -Sum","```","","**ForEach-Object:** Performs an operation on each object in a collection. (BEAWARE: Below command will prefix of","files/folder in the current dir)","","```powershell","Get-ChildItem | ForEach-Object { Rename-Item $_ -NewName \"Prefix_$_\" }","```","","**Sort-Object:** Sorts objects by specified properties.","","```powershell","Get-ChildItem | Sort-Object Length -Descending","```","","**Format-Table:** Formats output as a table with specified columns.","","```powershell","Get-Service | Format-Table -AutoSize  # ft alias","```","","**Format-List:** Formats output as a list of properties and values.","","```powershell","Get-Process | Format-List -Property Name, CPU  # fl alias","```","","### FileSystem {.col-span-2}","","```powershell","New-Item -path file.txt -type 'file' -value 'contents'","New-Item -path file.txt -type 'dir'","Copy-Item \u003csrc\u003e -destination \u003cdest\u003e","Move-Item -path  \u003csrc\u003e -destination \u003cdest\u003e","Remove-Item \u003cfile\u003e","Test-Path \u003cpath\u003e","Rename-Item -path \u003cpath\u003e -newname \u003cnewname\u003e","","# using .NET Base Class Library","[System.IO.File]::WriteAllText('test.txt', '')","[System.IO.File]::Delete('test.txt')","","Get-Content -Path \"test.txt\"","Get-Process | Out-File -FilePath \"processes.txt\"# Output to file","Get-Process | Export-Csv -Path \"processes.csv\"  # Output to csv","$data = Import-Csv -Path \"data.csv\"             # Import from csv","```","","## System Management","","### Windows Management Instrumentation {.col-span-2}","","```powershell","# Retrieve BIOS information","Get-CimInstance -ClassName Win32_BIOS","# Retrieve information about locally connected physical disk devices","Get-CimInstance -ClassName Win32_DiskDrive","# Retrieve information about install physical memory (RAM)","Get-CimInstance -ClassName Win32_PhysicalMemory","# Retrieve information about installed network adapters (physical + virtual)","Get-CimInstance -ClassName Win32_NetworkAdapter","# Retrieve information about installed graphics / video card (GPU)","Get-CimInstance -ClassName Win32_VideoController","","# List all the classNames","Get-CimClass | Select-Object -ExpandProperty CimClassName","# Explore the various WMI classes available in the root\\cimv2 namespace","Get-CimClass -Namespace root\\cimv2","# Explore the child WMI namespaces underneath the root\\cimv2 namespace","Get-CimInstance -Namespace root -ClassName __NAMESPACE","","","```","","### Network Management","","```powershell","# Test network connectivity to a remote host","Test-Connection -ComputerName google.com","","# Retrieve network adapter information","Get-NetAdapter","","# Retrieve IP address information","Get-NetIPAddress","","# Retrieve routing table information","Get-NetRoute","","# Test if a port is open on a remote host","Test-NetConnection google.com -Port 80","","```","","### User \u0026 Group Management {.col-span-2}","","```powershell","# Retrieve local user account information","Get-LocalUser","","# Create a new local user account","New-LocalUser -Name NewUser -Password (ConvertTo-SecureString \"Password123\" -AsPlainText -Force)","","# Remove a local user account","Remove-LocalUser -Name UserToRemove","","# Retrieve local group information","Get-LocalGroup","","# Add a member to a local group","Add-LocalGroupMember -Group Administrators -Member UserToAdd","```","","### Security \u0026 Permissions","","```powershell","# Retrieve access control lists for file/dir","Get-Acl C:\\Path\\To\\File.txt","","# Set access control lists for a file/dir","Set-Acl -Path C:\\Path\\To\\File.txt -AclObject $aclObject","```","","### Registry Management {.col-span-2}","","```powershell","# Retrieve registry key values","Get-ItemProperty -Path \"HKLM:\\Software\\Microsoft\\Windows\\CurrentVersion\\Uninstall\\*\" | Select DisplayName, DisplayVersion","","# Set registry key values","Set-ItemProperty -Path \"HKLM:\\Software\\MyApp\" -Name \"SettingName\" -Value \"NewValue\"","","# Create a new registry key value","New-ItemProperty -Path \"HKCU:\\Software\\MyApp\" -Name \"NewSetting\" -Value \"NewValue\" -PropertyType String","","# Remove a registry key value","Remove-ItemProperty -Path \"HKCU:\\Software\\MyApp\" -Name \"SettingToRemove\"","","# Check if a registry key exists","Test-Path \"HKLM:\\Software\\MyApp\"","```","","## Scripting","","### Variables {.col-span-2}","","Initializing a variable with/without a specified type:","","```powershell","$var = 0","[int] $var = 'Trevor'         # (throws an exception)","[string] $var = 'Trevor'      # (doesn't throw an exception)","$var.GetType()","","# Multiple Assigning","$a,$b,$c = 'a','b','c'","","# Create an array","$arrayvar = @('va1','va2')","","# Create dict","$dict = @{k1 = 'test'; k2 = 'best'}","```","","Variable Commands","","```powershell","New-Variable -Name FirstName -Value Trevor","New-Variable FirstName -Value Trevor -Option \u003cReadOnly/Constant\u003e","","Get-Variable","Get-Variable | ? { $PSItem.Options -contains 'constant' }","Get-Variable | ? { $PSItem.Options -contains 'readonly' }","","Remove-Variable -Name firstname","# Removes ReadOnly var","Remove-Variable -Name firstname -Force","```","","Variable types int32, int64, string, bool","","### Operators","","```powershell","# operators","# (a \u003cop\u003e b)","","= , += / -= , ++ / --","-eq / -ne , -lt / -gt , -le / -ge","","$FirstName = 'Trevor'","$FirstName -like 'T*'","$true; $false #bool true/false","","# ternary operator","$FoodToEat = $BaconIsYummy ? 'bacon' : 'beets'","","# -notin or -in","'Celery' -in @('Bacon', 'Sausage', 'Steak')","","# output: True","5 -is [int32]","","# regex match, array can be use","'Trevor' -match '^T\\w*'","","# Find multiple matches.","$regex = [regex]'(\\w*)'","$regex.Matches('this is test').Value","","```","","### Structure","","#### I/O operation","","```powershell","\"This displays a string\"","","Write-Host \"color\" -ForegroundColor Red","","$age = Read-host \"Enter age\"","","$pwd = Read-host \"password\" -asSecureString","","Clear-Host","```","","#### Flow Controls","","```powershell","IF(\u003c#Condition#\u003e){","\u003c#Commands#\u003e}ELSEIF(){}ELSE{}","","Switch($var){","\t\"val1\"{\u003c#Commands#\u003e; break}","    \"val2\"{\u003c#Commands#\u003e; break}","}","","For($ct=0;$ct -le 3;$ct++){}","","ForEach($var in $arr){}","","while($var -ne 0){}","","Do{}While()","","```","","### Function / Modules {.row-span-2}","","#### Example 1","","```powershell","function funcname{","","    [CmdletBinding()]","\tparam(","\t\t[Parameter(Mandatory)]","\t\t[String]$user","\t)","\tWrite-Host \"welcome \" $user","    return \"value\"","}","$var = funcname -user pcb","```","","#### Example 2","","```powershell","function Get-EvenNumbers {","    [CmdletBinding()]","    param (","        [Parameter(ValueFromPipeline = $true)]","        [int] $Number","    )","    begin {\u003c#command#\u003e}","    process {","        if ($Number % 2 -eq 0) {","            Write-Output $Number","        }","    }","    end {\u003c#command#\u003e}","}","1..10 | Get-EvenNumbers","","```","","#### Modules","","```powershell","# PowerShell looks module in the path","$env:PSModulePath","","# lists all modules installed on system","Get-Module -ListAvailable","# modules imported into current session","Get-Module","","Import-Module \u003cmoduleName\u003e","Remove-Module \u003cmoduleName\u003e","","Find-Module -Tag cloud","Find-Module -Name ps*","","# Create an in-memory PowerShell module","New-Module -Name trevor -ScriptBlock {","  function Add($a,$b) { $a + $b } }","","","```","","### Tips","","- In most of the languages, escape character is backslash **\\\\** whereas in PowerShell it is backtick **`**","","```powershell","","```","","## Also see <div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Basic Commands</h2><a id="user-content-basic-commands" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Basic Commands" href="#basic-commands"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Helper Commands</h3><a id="user-content-helper-commands" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Helper Commands" href="#helper-commands"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong><em>Powershell follows Verb-Noun format for their commands.</em></strong></p>
<p dir="auto">Some common Verbs:</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Verb</th>
<th>Description</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>Get</td>
<td>Used to retrieve information.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Set</td>
<td>Used to configure or change settings.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>New</td>
<td>Used to create new instances of objects.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Remove</td>
<td>Used to delete or remove items.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Invoke</td>
<td>Used to execute a specific action or operation.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Start</td>
<td>Used to initiate a process or operation.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Stop</td>
<td>Used to halt or terminate a process or operation.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Enable</td>
<td>Used to activate or enable a feature.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Disable</td>
<td>Used to deactivate or disable a feature.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Test</td>
<td>Used to perform tests or checks.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Update</td>
<td>Used to update or refresh data or configurations.</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">Lists available modules</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Get-Module --ListAvailable"><pre><span class="pl-c1">Get-Module</span> <span class="pl-k">--</span>ListAvailable</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Lists available cmdlets and functions.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Get-Command -Module ActiveDirectory"><pre><span class="pl-c1">Get-Command</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Module ActiveDirectory</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Retrieves help</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Get-Help &lt;cmd&gt;
Get-Help &lt;cmd&gt; -Examples
Get-Help -Name Get-Process -Parameter Id"><pre><span class="pl-c1">Get-Help</span> <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>cmd<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Get-Help</span> <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>cmd<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Examples
<span class="pl-c1">Get-Help</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Name <span class="pl-c1">Get-Process</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Parameter Id</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Lists aliases and their corresponding cmdlet names.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Get-Alias | Select-Object Name, Definition"><pre><span class="pl-c1">Get-Alias</span> <span class="pl-k">|</span> <span class="pl-c1">Select-Object</span> Name<span class="pl-k">,</span> Definition</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Get-Member:</strong> Displays the properties and methods of objects.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Get-Process | Get-Member"><pre><span class="pl-c1">Get-Process</span> <span class="pl-k">|</span> <span class="pl-c1">Get-Member</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Object Manipulation {.col-span-2}</h3><a id="user-content-object-manipulation-col-span-2" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Object Manipulation {.col-span-2}" href="#object-manipulation-col-span-2"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Select-Object:</strong> Selects specific properties from objects or customizes their display.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Get-Process | Select-Object Name, CPU"><pre><span class="pl-c1">Get-Process</span> <span class="pl-k">|</span> <span class="pl-c1">Select-Object</span> Name<span class="pl-k">,</span> CPU</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Where-Object:</strong> Filters objects based on specified conditions.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Get-Service | Where-Object { $PSItem.Status -eq 'Running' }
#OR
Get-Service | ? { $_.Status -eq 'Running' }"><pre><span class="pl-c1">Get-Service</span> <span class="pl-k">|</span> <span class="pl-c1">Where-Object</span> { <span class="pl-c1">$PSItem<span class="pl-smi">.Status</span></span> <span class="pl-k">-eq</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>Running<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> }
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span>OR</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Get-Service</span> <span class="pl-k">|</span> <span class="pl-k">?</span> { <span class="pl-c1">$_<span class="pl-smi">.Status</span></span> <span class="pl-k">-eq</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>Running<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> }</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Measure-Object:</strong> Calculates statistics, like sum, average, and count, for object properties.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Get-Process | Measure-Object -Property WorkingSet -Sum"><pre><span class="pl-c1">Get-Process</span> <span class="pl-k">|</span> <span class="pl-c1">Measure-Object</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Property WorkingSet <span class="pl-k">-</span>Sum</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>ForEach-Object:</strong> Performs an operation on each object in a collection. (BEAWARE: Below command will prefix of
files/folder in the current dir)</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Get-ChildItem | ForEach-Object { Rename-Item $_ -NewName &quot;Prefix_$_&quot; }"><pre><span class="pl-c1">Get-ChildItem</span> <span class="pl-k">|</span> <span class="pl-c1">ForEach-Object</span> { <span class="pl-c1">Rename-Item</span> <span class="pl-c1">$_</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>NewName <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Prefix_<span class="pl-c1">$_</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> }</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Sort-Object:</strong> Sorts objects by specified properties.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Get-ChildItem | Sort-Object Length -Descending"><pre><span class="pl-c1">Get-ChildItem</span> <span class="pl-k">|</span> <span class="pl-c1">Sort-Object</span> Length <span class="pl-k">-</span>Descending</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Format-Table:</strong> Formats output as a table with specified columns.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Get-Service | Format-Table -AutoSize  # ft alias"><pre><span class="pl-c1">Get-Service</span> <span class="pl-k">|</span> <span class="pl-c1">Format-Table</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>AutoSize  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> ft alias</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Format-List:</strong> Formats output as a list of properties and values.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Get-Process | Format-List -Property Name, CPU  # fl alias"><pre><span class="pl-c1">Get-Process</span> <span class="pl-k">|</span> <span class="pl-c1">Format-List</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Property Name<span class="pl-k">,</span> CPU  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> fl alias</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">FileSystem {.col-span-2}</h3><a id="user-content-filesystem-col-span-2" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: FileSystem {.col-span-2}" href="#filesystem-col-span-2"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="New-Item -path file.txt -type 'file' -value 'contents'
New-Item -path file.txt -type 'dir'
Copy-Item &lt;src&gt; -destination &lt;dest&gt;
Move-Item -path  &lt;src&gt; -destination &lt;dest&gt;
Remove-Item &lt;file&gt;
Test-Path &lt;path&gt;
Rename-Item -path &lt;path&gt; -newname &lt;newname&gt;

# using .NET Base Class Library
[System.IO.File]::WriteAllText('test.txt', '')
[System.IO.File]::Delete('test.txt')

Get-Content -Path &quot;test.txt&quot;
Get-Process | Out-File -FilePath &quot;processes.txt&quot;# Output to file
Get-Process | Export-Csv -Path &quot;processes.csv&quot;  # Output to csv
$data = Import-Csv -Path &quot;data.csv&quot;             # Import from csv"><pre><span class="pl-c1">New-Item</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>path file.txt <span class="pl-k">-</span>type <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>file<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>value <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>contents<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>
<span class="pl-c1">New-Item</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>path file.txt <span class="pl-k">-</span>type <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>dir<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>
<span class="pl-c1">Copy-Item</span> <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>src<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>destination <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>dest<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Move-Item</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>path  <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>src<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>destination <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>dest<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Remove-Item</span> <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>file<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Test-Path</span> <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>path<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Rename-Item</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>path <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>path<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>newname <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>newname<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> using .NET Base Class Library</span>
[<span class="pl-k">System.IO.File</span>]::WriteAllText(<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>test.txt<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span><span class="pl-k">,</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span><span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>)
[<span class="pl-k">System.IO.File</span>]::Delete(<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>test.txt<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>)

<span class="pl-c1">Get-Content</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Path <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>test.txt<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-c1">Get-Process</span> <span class="pl-k">|</span> <span class="pl-c1">Out-File</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>FilePath <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>processes.txt<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Output to file</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Get-Process</span> <span class="pl-k">|</span> <span class="pl-c1">Export-Csv</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Path <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>processes.csv<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Output to csv</span>
<span class="pl-smi">$data</span> <span class="pl-k">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">Import-Csv</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Path <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>data.csv<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>             <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Import from csv</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">System Management</h2><a id="user-content-system-management" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: System Management" href="#system-management"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Windows Management Instrumentation {.col-span-2}</h3><a id="user-content-windows-management-instrumentation-col-span-2" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Windows Management Instrumentation {.col-span-2}" href="#windows-management-instrumentation-col-span-2"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Retrieve BIOS information
Get-CimInstance -ClassName Win32_BIOS
# Retrieve information about locally connected physical disk devices
Get-CimInstance -ClassName Win32_DiskDrive
# Retrieve information about install physical memory (RAM)
Get-CimInstance -ClassName Win32_PhysicalMemory
# Retrieve information about installed network adapters (physical + virtual)
Get-CimInstance -ClassName Win32_NetworkAdapter
# Retrieve information about installed graphics / video card (GPU)
Get-CimInstance -ClassName Win32_VideoController

# List all the classNames
Get-CimClass | Select-Object -ExpandProperty CimClassName
# Explore the various WMI classes available in the root\cimv2 namespace
Get-CimClass -Namespace root\cimv2
# Explore the child WMI namespaces underneath the root\cimv2 namespace
Get-CimInstance -Namespace root -ClassName __NAMESPACE

"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Retrieve BIOS information</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Get-CimInstance</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>ClassName Win32_BIOS
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Retrieve information about locally connected physical disk devices</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Get-CimInstance</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>ClassName Win32_DiskDrive
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Retrieve information about install physical memory (RAM)</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Get-CimInstance</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>ClassName Win32_PhysicalMemory
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Retrieve information about installed network adapters (physical + virtual)</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Get-CimInstance</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>ClassName Win32_NetworkAdapter
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Retrieve information about installed graphics / video card (GPU)</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Get-CimInstance</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>ClassName Win32_VideoController

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> List all the classNames</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Get-CimClass</span> <span class="pl-k">|</span> <span class="pl-c1">Select-Object</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>ExpandProperty CimClassName
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Explore the various WMI classes available in the root\cimv2 namespace</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Get-CimClass</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Namespace root\cimv2
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Explore the child WMI namespaces underneath the root\cimv2 namespace</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Get-CimInstance</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Namespace root <span class="pl-k">-</span>ClassName __NAMESPACE

</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Network Management</h3><a id="user-content-network-management" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Network Management" href="#network-management"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Test network connectivity to a remote host
Test-Connection -ComputerName google.com

# Retrieve network adapter information
Get-NetAdapter

# Retrieve IP address information
Get-NetIPAddress

# Retrieve routing table information
Get-NetRoute

# Test if a port is open on a remote host
Test-NetConnection google.com -Port 80
"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Test network connectivity to a remote host</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Test-Connection</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>ComputerName <span class="pl-c1">google.com</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Retrieve network adapter information</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Get-NetAdapter</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Retrieve IP address information</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Get-NetIPAddress</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Retrieve routing table information</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Get-NetRoute</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Test if a port is open on a remote host</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Test-NetConnection</span> <span class="pl-c1">google.com</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Port <span class="pl-c1">80</span>
</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">User &amp; Group Management {.col-span-2}</h3><a id="user-content-user--group-management-col-span-2" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: User &amp; Group Management {.col-span-2}" href="#user--group-management-col-span-2"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Retrieve local user account information
Get-LocalUser

# Create a new local user account
New-LocalUser -Name NewUser -Password (ConvertTo-SecureString &quot;Password123&quot; -AsPlainText -Force)

# Remove a local user account
Remove-LocalUser -Name UserToRemove

# Retrieve local group information
Get-LocalGroup

# Add a member to a local group
Add-LocalGroupMember -Group Administrators -Member UserToAdd"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Retrieve local user account information</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Get-LocalUser</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Create a new local user account</span>
<span class="pl-c1">New-LocalUser</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Name NewUser <span class="pl-k">-</span>Password (<span class="pl-c1">ConvertTo-SecureString</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Password123<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>AsPlainText <span class="pl-k">-</span>Force)

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Remove a local user account</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Remove-LocalUser</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Name UserToRemove

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Retrieve local group information</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Get-LocalGroup</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Add a member to a local group</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Add-LocalGroupMember</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Group Administrators <span class="pl-k">-</span>Member UserToAdd</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Security &amp; Permissions</h3><a id="user-content-security--permissions" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Security &amp; Permissions" href="#security--permissions"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Retrieve access control lists for file/dir
Get-Acl C:\Path\To\File.txt

# Set access control lists for a file/dir
Set-Acl -Path C:\Path\To\File.txt -AclObject $aclObject"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Retrieve access control lists for file/dir</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Get-Acl</span> C:\Path\To\File.txt

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Set access control lists for a file/dir</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Set-Acl</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Path C:\Path\To\File.txt <span class="pl-k">-</span>AclObject <span class="pl-smi">$aclObject</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Registry Management {.col-span-2}</h3><a id="user-content-registry-management-col-span-2" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Registry Management {.col-span-2}" href="#registry-management-col-span-2"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Retrieve registry key values
Get-ItemProperty -Path &quot;HKLM:\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Uninstall\*&quot; | Select DisplayName, DisplayVersion

# Set registry key values
Set-ItemProperty -Path &quot;HKLM:\Software\MyApp&quot; -Name &quot;SettingName&quot; -Value &quot;NewValue&quot;

# Create a new registry key value
New-ItemProperty -Path &quot;HKCU:\Software\MyApp&quot; -Name &quot;NewSetting&quot; -Value &quot;NewValue&quot; -PropertyType String

# Remove a registry key value
Remove-ItemProperty -Path &quot;HKCU:\Software\MyApp&quot; -Name &quot;SettingToRemove&quot;

# Check if a registry key exists
Test-Path &quot;HKLM:\Software\MyApp&quot;"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Retrieve registry key values</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Get-ItemProperty</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Path <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>HKLM:\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Uninstall\*<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> <span class="pl-k">|</span> Select DisplayName<span class="pl-k">,</span> DisplayVersion

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Set registry key values</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Set-ItemProperty</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Path <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>HKLM:\Software\MyApp<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Name <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>SettingName<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Value <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>NewValue<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Create a new registry key value</span>
<span class="pl-c1">New-ItemProperty</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Path <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>HKCU:\Software\MyApp<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Name <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>NewSetting<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Value <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>NewValue<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>PropertyType String

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Remove a registry key value</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Remove-ItemProperty</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Path <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>HKCU:\Software\MyApp<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Name <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>SettingToRemove<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Check if a registry key exists</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Test-Path</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>HKLM:\Software\MyApp<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Scripting</h2><a id="user-content-scripting" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Scripting" href="#scripting"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Variables {.col-span-2}</h3><a id="user-content-variables-col-span-2" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Variables {.col-span-2}" href="#variables-col-span-2"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Initializing a variable with/without a specified type:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="$var = 0
[int] $var = 'Trevor'         # (throws an exception)
[string] $var = 'Trevor'      # (doesn't throw an exception)
$var.GetType()

# Multiple Assigning
$a,$b,$c = 'a','b','c'

# Create an array
$arrayvar = @('va1','va2')

# Create dict
$dict = @{k1 = 'test'; k2 = 'best'}"><pre><span class="pl-smi">$var</span> <span class="pl-k">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">0</span>
[<span class="pl-k">int</span>] <span class="pl-smi">$var</span> <span class="pl-k">=</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>Trevor<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>         <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> (throws an exception)</span>
[<span class="pl-k">string</span>] <span class="pl-smi">$var</span> <span class="pl-k">=</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>Trevor<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>      <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> (doesn't throw an exception)</span>
<span class="pl-smi">$var<span class="pl-smi">.GetType</span></span>()

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Multiple Assigning</span>
<span class="pl-smi">$a</span><span class="pl-k">,</span><span class="pl-smi">$b</span><span class="pl-k">,</span><span class="pl-smi">$c</span> <span class="pl-k">=</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>a<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span><span class="pl-k">,</span><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>b<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span><span class="pl-k">,</span><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>c<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Create an array</span>
<span class="pl-smi">$arrayvar</span> <span class="pl-k">=</span> <span class="pl-k">@</span>(<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>va1<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span><span class="pl-k">,</span><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>va2<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>)

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Create dict</span>
<span class="pl-smi">$dict</span> <span class="pl-k">=</span> <span class="pl-k">@</span>{<span class="pl-smi">k1</span> <span class="pl-k">=</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>test<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>; <span class="pl-smi">k2</span> <span class="pl-k">=</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>best<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Variable Commands</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="New-Variable -Name FirstName -Value Trevor
New-Variable FirstName -Value Trevor -Option &lt;ReadOnly/Constant&gt;

Get-Variable
Get-Variable | ? { $PSItem.Options -contains 'constant' }
Get-Variable | ? { $PSItem.Options -contains 'readonly' }

Remove-Variable -Name firstname
# Removes ReadOnly var
Remove-Variable -Name firstname -Force"><pre><span class="pl-c1">New-Variable</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Name FirstName <span class="pl-k">-</span>Value Trevor
<span class="pl-c1">New-Variable</span> FirstName <span class="pl-k">-</span>Value Trevor <span class="pl-k">-</span>Option <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>ReadOnly<span class="pl-k">/</span>Constant<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>

<span class="pl-c1">Get-Variable</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Get-Variable</span> <span class="pl-k">|</span> <span class="pl-k">?</span> { <span class="pl-c1">$PSItem<span class="pl-smi">.Options</span></span> <span class="pl-k">-contains</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>constant<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> }
<span class="pl-c1">Get-Variable</span> <span class="pl-k">|</span> <span class="pl-k">?</span> { <span class="pl-c1">$PSItem<span class="pl-smi">.Options</span></span> <span class="pl-k">-contains</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>readonly<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> }

<span class="pl-c1">Remove-Variable</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Name firstname
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Removes ReadOnly var</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Remove-Variable</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Name firstname <span class="pl-k">-</span>Force</pre></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Operators</h3><a id="user-content-operators" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Operators" href="#operators"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# operators
# (a &lt;op&gt; b)

= , += / -= , ++ / --
-eq / -ne , -lt / -gt , -le / -ge

$FirstName = 'Trevor'
$FirstName -like 'T*'
$true; $false #bool true/false

# ternary operator
$FoodToEat = $BaconIsYummy ? 'bacon' : 'beets'

# -notin or -in
'Celery' -in @('Bacon', 'Sausage', 'Steak')

# output: True
5 -is [int32]

# regex match, array can be use
'Trevor' -match '^T\w*'

# Find multiple matches.
$regex = [regex]'(\w*)'
$regex.Matches('this is test').Value
"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> operators</span>
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> (a &lt;op&gt; b)</span>

<span class="pl-k">=</span> <span class="pl-k">,</span> <span class="pl-k">+=</span> <span class="pl-k">/</span> <span class="pl-k">-=</span> <span class="pl-k">,</span> <span class="pl-k">++</span> <span class="pl-k">/</span> <span class="pl-k">--</span>
<span class="pl-k">-eq</span> <span class="pl-k">/</span> <span class="pl-k">-ne</span> <span class="pl-k">,</span> <span class="pl-k">-lt</span> <span class="pl-k">/</span> <span class="pl-k">-gt</span> <span class="pl-k">,</span> <span class="pl-k">-le</span> <span class="pl-k">/</span> <span class="pl-k">-ge</span>

<span class="pl-smi">$FirstName</span> <span class="pl-k">=</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>Trevor<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>
<span class="pl-smi">$FirstName</span> <span class="pl-k">-like</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>T*<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>
<span class="pl-c1">$true</span>; <span class="pl-c1">$false</span> <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span>bool true/false</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> ternary operator</span>
<span class="pl-smi">$FoodToEat</span> <span class="pl-k">=</span> <span class="pl-smi">$BaconIsYummy</span> <span class="pl-k">?</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>bacon<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> : <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>beets<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> -notin or -in</span>
<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>Celery<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> <span class="pl-k">-in</span> <span class="pl-k">@</span>(<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>Bacon<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span><span class="pl-k">,</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>Sausage<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span><span class="pl-k">,</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>Steak<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>)

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> output: True</span>
<span class="pl-c1">5</span> <span class="pl-k">-is</span> [<span class="pl-k">int32</span>]

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> regex match, array can be use</span>
<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>Trevor<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> <span class="pl-k">-match</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>^T\w*<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Find multiple matches.</span>
<span class="pl-smi">$regex</span> <span class="pl-k">=</span> [<span class="pl-k">regex</span>]<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>(\w*)<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>
<span class="pl-smi">$regex<span class="pl-smi">.Matches</span></span>(<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>this is test<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>).Value
</pre></div>

<span class="pl-c1">$pwd</span> <span class="pl-k">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">Read-host</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>password<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>asSecureString

<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="IF(&lt;#Condition#&gt;){
&lt;#Commands#&gt;}ELSEIF(){}ELSE{}

Switch($var){
	&quot;val1&quot;{&lt;#Commands#&gt;; break}
    &quot;val2&quot;{&lt;#Commands#&gt;; break}
}

For($ct=0;$ct -le 3;$ct++){}

ForEach($var in $arr){}

while($var -ne 0){}

Do{}While()
"><pre><span class="pl-k">IF</span>(<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;#</span>Condition<span class="pl-c">#&gt;</span></span>){
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;#</span>Commands<span class="pl-c">#&gt;</span></span>}<span class="pl-k">ELSEIF</span>(){}<span class="pl-k">ELSE</span>{}

<span class="pl-k">Switch</span>(<span class="pl-smi">$var</span>){
	<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>val1<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>{<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;#</span>Commands<span class="pl-c">#&gt;</span></span>; <span class="pl-k">break</span>}
    <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>val2<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>{<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;#</span>Commands<span class="pl-c">#&gt;</span></span>; <span class="pl-k">break</span>}
}

<span class="pl-k">For</span>(<span class="pl-smi">$ct</span><span class="pl-k">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>;<span class="pl-smi">$ct</span> <span class="pl-k">-le</span> <span class="pl-c1">3</span>;<span class="pl-smi">$ct</span><span class="pl-k">++</span>){}

<span class="pl-k">ForEach</span>(<span class="pl-smi">$var</span> <span class="pl-k">in</span> <span class="pl-smi">$arr</span>){}

<span class="pl-k">while</span>(<span class="pl-smi">$var</span> <span class="pl-k">-ne</span> <span class="pl-c1">0</span>){}

<span class="pl-k">Do</span>{}<span class="pl-k">While</span>()
</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Function / Modules {.row-span-2}</h3><a id="user-content-function--modules-row-span-2" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Function / Modules {.row-span-2}" href="#function--modules-row-span-2"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Example 1</h4><a id="user-content-example-1" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Example 1" href="#example-1"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="function funcname{

    [CmdletBinding()]
	param(
		[Parameter(Mandatory)]
		[String]$user
	)
	Write-Host &quot;welcome &quot; $user
    return &quot;value&quot;
}
$var = funcname -user pcb"><pre><span class="pl-k">function</span> <span class="pl-en">funcname</span>{

    [<span class="pl-c1">CmdletBinding</span>()]
	<span class="pl-k">param</span>(
		[<span class="pl-c1">Parameter</span>(<span class="pl-v">Mandatory</span>)]
		[<span class="pl-k">String</span>]<span class="pl-smi">$user</span>
	)
	<span class="pl-c1">Write-Host</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>welcome <span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> <span class="pl-smi">$user</span>
    <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>value<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
}
<span class="pl-smi">$var</span> <span class="pl-k">=</span> funcname <span class="pl-k">-</span>user pcb</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Example 2</h4><a id="user-content-example-2" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Example 2" href="#example-2"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="function Get-EvenNumbers {
    [CmdletBinding()]
    param (
        [Parameter(ValueFromPipeline = $true)]
        [int] $Number
    )
    begin {&lt;#command#&gt;}
    process {
        if ($Number % 2 -eq 0) {
            Write-Output $Number
        }
    }
    end {&lt;#command#&gt;}
}
1..10 | Get-EvenNumbers
"><pre><span class="pl-k">function</span> <span class="pl-en">Get-EvenNumbers</span> {
    [<span class="pl-c1">CmdletBinding</span>()]
    <span class="pl-k">param</span> (
        [<span class="pl-c1">Parameter</span>(<span class="pl-v">ValueFromPipeline</span> <span class="pl-k">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">$true</span>)]
        [<span class="pl-k">int</span>] <span class="pl-smi">$Number</span>
    )
    <span class="pl-k">begin</span> {<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;#</span>command<span class="pl-c">#&gt;</span></span>}
    <span class="pl-k">process</span> {
        <span class="pl-k">if</span> (<span class="pl-smi">$Number</span> <span class="pl-k">%</span> <span class="pl-c1">2</span> <span class="pl-k">-eq</span> <span class="pl-c1">0</span>) {
            <span class="pl-c1">Write-Output</span> <span class="pl-smi">$Number</span>
        }
    }
    <span class="pl-k">end</span> {<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;#</span>command<span class="pl-c">#&gt;</span></span>}
}
<span class="pl-c1">1</span><span class="pl-k">..</span><span class="pl-c1">10</span> <span class="pl-k">|</span> <span class="pl-c1">Get-EvenNumbers</span>
</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Modules</h4><a id="user-content-modules" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Modules" href="#modules"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# PowerShell looks module in the path
$env:PSModulePath

# lists all modules installed on system
Get-Module -ListAvailable
# modules imported into current session
Get-Module

Import-Module &lt;moduleName&gt;
Remove-Module &lt;moduleName&gt;

Find-Module -Tag cloud
Find-Module -Name ps*

# Create an in-memory PowerShell module
New-Module -Name trevor -ScriptBlock {
  function Add($a,$b) { $a + $b } }

"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> PowerShell looks module in the path</span>
<span class="pl-smi">$<span class="pl-c1">env:</span>PSModulePath</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> lists all modules installed on system</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Get-Module</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>ListAvailable
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> modules imported into current session</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Get-Module</span>

<span class="pl-c1">Import-Module</span> <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>moduleName<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-c1">Remove-Module</span> <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>moduleName<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>

<span class="pl-c1">Find-Module</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Tag cloud
<span class="pl-c1">Find-Module</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Name ps<span class="pl-k">*</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Create an in-memory PowerShell module</span>
<span class="pl-c1">New-Module</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Name trevor <span class="pl-k">-</span>ScriptBlock {
  <span class="pl-k">function</span> <span class="pl-en">Add</span>(<span class="pl-smi">$a</span><span class="pl-k">,</span><span class="pl-smi">$b</span>) { <span class="pl-smi">$a</span> <span class="pl-k">+</span> <span class="pl-smi">$b</span> } }

</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Tips</h3><a id="user-content-tips" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Tips" href="#tips"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>In most of the languages, escape character is backslash <strong>\</strong> whereas in PowerShell it is backtick <strong>`</strong></li>
</ul>
